//Strict optimizations enabled
//28 elements
rule ("loading pause") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        LoadingPause;
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        If(Compare(Global.start, ==, False)); // 5 elements
            Wait Until(And(Compare(Server Load, <, 180), Compare(Server Load Average, <, 180)), 10); // 12 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 200), 2); // 6 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//207 elements
rule ("decode text list") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        decompressTextSub;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(textGroup, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String("")); // 5 elements
        Set Global Variable(decodeString, 0); // 2 elements
        While(Compare(Global.decodeString, <, Count Of(Global.decodeIn))); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(decodePosition, 0); // 2 elements
            While(Compare(Global.decodePosition, <, String Length(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, Global.decodeString)))); // 10 elements
                Set Global Variable(textToken, Index Of String Char(Global.decodeTable, String Slice(Value In Array(Global.decodeIn, Global.decodeString), Global.decodePosition, 1))); // 13 elements
                If(Compare(Global.textToken, ==, 0)); // 6 elements
                    Set Global Variable At Index(textGroup, Count Of(Global.textGroup), If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.textItem, ==, Custom String("")), Null, String Slice(Global.textItem, 0, String Length(Global.textItem)))); // 21 elements
                    Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String("")); // 5 elements
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 53), ==, True)); // 8 elements
                        Set Global Variable At Index(infoText, Subtract(Count Of(Global.textGroup), 1), Last Of(Global.textGroup)); // 8 elements
                        Wait(0.15, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                Else If(Compare(Global.textToken, ==, 1)); // 6 elements
                    Set Global Variable At Index(decodeOut, Count Of(Global.decodeOut), Global.textGroup); // 4 elements
                    Set Global Variable(textGroup, Empty Array); // 1 element
                Else; // 1 element
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 51, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 49), Round To Integer(Divide(Global.textToken, 62), Down))); // 14 elements
                    Set Global Variable(textItem, Custom String("{0}{1}", Global.textItem, String Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 51), Multiply(Modulo(Global.textToken, 62), 2), If-Then-Else(Compare(String Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 51), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Global.textToken, 62), 2), 1), 1), ==, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 50)), 1, 2)))); // 50 elements
                    If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 53), ==, True)); // 8 elements
                        Set Global Variable At Index(infoText, Count Of(Global.textGroup), Global.textItem); // 4 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                If(Not(Modulo(Global.decodePosition, 12))); // 6 elements
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(decodePosition, Add, 1); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(decodeString, Add, 1); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        If(Compare(Count Of(Global.decodeOut), ==, 0)); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(decodeOut, Global.textGroup); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//119 elements
rule ("start hero counter damage") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        StartCounterMods;
    }
    actions {
        For Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot, 0, Count Of((Event Player).counterMods), 1); // 6 elements
            Stop Damage Modification(Value In Array((Event Player).counterMods, (Event Player).counterSlot)); // 7 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Empty Array); // 1 element
        If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 16), ==, True)); // 9 elements
            Start Damage Modification(All Players(Team 2), Event Player, If-Then-Else(Compare(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 11), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Players In Slot(Subtract(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1)))), 3), Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Event Player))))), >, 100), 150, 100), Receivers Damagers and Damage Percent); // 44 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Append To Array, Last Damage Modification ID); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            For Player Variable(Event Player, counterSlot, 0, 3, 1); // 4 elements
                Start Damage Modification(Filtered Array(All Players(Team 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Current Array Element))), ==, Evaluate Once((Event Player).counterSlot))), Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 11), Add(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.counterHeroType, Index Of Array Value(Global.hero, Hero Of(Event Player))), 3), Evaluate Once((Event Player).counterSlot))), Receivers Damagers and Damage Percent); // 41 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, counterMods, Append To Array, Last Damage Modification ID); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("hero counters: damage players do to zombies") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 16, False); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(StartCounterMods); // 1 element
    }
}

//17 elements
rule ("hero counters: damage followers do to zombies") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Slot Of(Event Player) >= 4; // 3 elements
        Slot Of(Event Player) <= 7; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).isMonkeyBomb != True; // 3 elements
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 16, True); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(StartCounterMods); // 1 element
    }
}

//20 elements
rule ("global bank interest gain every round") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.numberOfZombies <= 0; // 3 elements
        Global.bankPoints > 0; // 3 elements
        Global.start == 1; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, Multiply(Global.bankPoints, Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_InterestRate, Global.bankTier))); // 8 elements
    }
}

//70 elements
rule ("global bank tier upgrade") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements
        Distance Between(Event Player, First Of(Global.bankButtons)) < 1; // 6 elements
        Global.bankTier < Subtract(Count Of(Global.bankTiers_Cost), 1); // 7 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Global.bankPoints, >=, Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_Cost, Add(Global.bankTier, 1)))); // 12 elements
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Subtract, Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_Cost, Add(Global.bankTier, 1))); // 8 elements
            Modify Global Variable(bankTier, Add, 1); // 2 elements
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("You have permanently upgraded your bank to tier {0}", Global.bankTier)); // 8 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70); // 5 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("You need {0} points in the bank to upgrade!", Value In Array(Global.bankTiers_Cost, Add(Global.bankTier, 1)))); // 14 elements
    }
}

//85 elements
rule ("global bank withdraw button") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 1)) < 1; // 8 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart, Total Time Elapsed); // 1 element
        While(Compare(Global.bankPoints, >, 0)); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep, Min(If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 2), 250, If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 5), 1000, 10000)), Global.bankPoints)); // 29 elements
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Subtract, (Event Player).bankStep); // 3 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, (Event Player).bankStep); // 3 elements
            Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, points2); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, (Event Player).points); // 3 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).withdrawNotif, ==, 0)); // 7 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, withdrawNotif, 1); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("The bank is out of funds!")); // 5 elements
    }
}

//87 elements
rule ("global bank deposit button") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 2)) < 1; // 8 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankHoldStart, Total Time Elapsed); // 1 element
        While(Compare((Event Player).points, >, 0)); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, bankStep, Min(If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 2), 200, If-Then-Else(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).bankHoldStart), <, 5), 1000, 10000)), (Event Player).points)); // 30 elements
            Modify Global Variable(bankPoints, Add, (Event Player).bankStep); // 3 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, (Event Player).bankStep); // 3 elements
            Stop Chasing Player Variable(Event Player, points2); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, points2, (Event Player).points); // 3 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).depNotif, ==, 0)); // 7 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, depNotif, 1); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You don't have enough points to deposit!")); // 5 elements
    }
}

//116 elements
rule ("credit card system") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements
        Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.bankButtons, 4)) < 1; // 8 elements
        (Event Player).creditTier < Subtract(Count Of(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost), 1); // 8 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, Add((Event Player).creditTier, 1)))); // 14 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, Add((Event Player).creditTier, 1))); // 9 elements
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), 1000, Destination and Rate); // 6 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, creditTier, Add, 1); // 2 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, creditcardlimit, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Limit, (Event Player).creditTier)); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, cashback, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cashback, (Event Player).creditTier)); // 6 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Your credit card is now tier {0}", (Event Player).creditTier)); // 7 elements
            Wait(4, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Hold spacebar to see credit card details")); // 5 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).creditTier, ==, 0)); // 7 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You need {0} points buy a credit card!", Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, 1))); // 9 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You need {0} points to upgrade!", Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_Cost, Add((Event Player).creditTier, 1)))); // 13 elements
    }
}

//45 elements
rule ("credit card interest") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).points < 0; // 4 elements
        Global.numberOfZombies < 1; // 3 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).creditTier > 0; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 48, Multiply((Event Player).points, Value In Array(Global.creditCardTiers_InterestRate, (Event Player).creditTier))); // 11 elements
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Add, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 48)); // 5 elements
        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), 1000, Destination and Rate); // 6 elements
        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You have incurred {0} interest on your credit card.", Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 48))); // 9 elements
    }
}

//173 elements
rule ("2 round change") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.numberOfZombies <= 0; // 3 elements
        Global.start == 1; // 3 elements
        Is True For Any(All Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).outofpsawn, ==, 1)) == True; // 11 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.16, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, Count Of(Global.omnicspawn), 1); // 5 elements
            Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Value In Array(Global.omnicspawn, Global.forindex)); // 6 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Modify Global Variable(roundnumber, Add, 1); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 28, 1); // 3 elements
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), String("Round {0}", Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 62), Global.romanNcounter))); // 15 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 23, False); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(roundRespawns, Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))); // 7 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 14, Random Value In Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))))); // 9 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global.roundRespawns, perk, 5, 0); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Global.roundRespawns, _extendedPlayerCollection, 15, Empty Array); // 3 elements
        Resurrect(Global.roundRespawns); // 2 elements
        Clear Status(Global.roundRespawns, Burning); // 2 elements
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 14), !=, Null)); // 8 elements
            Teleport(Global.roundRespawns, Position Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 14))); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Global.roundRespawns, doorSkipCheck, True); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Global.roundRespawns, Hero(Ashe)); // 3 elements
        Resurrect(Filtered Array(All Dead Players(Team 1), Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))); // 6 elements
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, 1); // 4 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global.forindex, Vector(500, Empty Array, 500)); // 7 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 51, False); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 34, False); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 50, False); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(All Players(Team 1), _extendedPlayerCollection, 49, False); // 4 elements
        Set Status(All Living Players(Team 1), Null, Phased Out, 2); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), monkeyBombsLeft, 3); // 4 elements
        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 37, Add, 1); // 3 elements
        If(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <, 12)); // 6 elements
            Modify Global Variable(romanNcounter, Add, 1); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 38, Random Integer(1, 5)); // 6 elements
        Modify Global Variable(costmultiplier, Multiply, Random Real(1, 1)); // 5 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 115, Random Real(1.01, 1.03)); // 6 elements
    }
}

    }
    actions {
        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombsLeft, Subtract, 1); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, True); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombSlot, Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4)); // 5 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 21, Entity Exists(Players In Slot((Event Player).monkeyBombSlot, Team 1))); // 8 elements
        Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 1, (Event Player).monkeyBombSlot); // 4 elements

        Wait(0.03, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements

        Set Player Variable((Event Player).monkeyBomb, isMonkeyBomb, True); // 3 elements

        Attach Players((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Event Player, Vector(Empty Array, Empty Array, 2)); // 7 elements
        Start Scaling Player((Event Player).monkeyBomb, 0.2, True); // 4 elements
        Start Modifying Hero Voice Lines((Event Player).monkeyBomb, 1.3, True); // 4 elements
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Detach Players((Event Player).monkeyBomb); // 3 elements
        Wait(0.2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Press Button((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Button(Jump)); // 4 elements
        Apply Impulse((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Vector Towards(Event Player, (Event Player).monkeyBomb), 25, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion); // 8 elements
        Create Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Aura, Color(Orange), (Event Player).monkeyBomb, 0.5, Visible To Position and Radius); // 7 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 23, Last Created Entity); // 2 elements

        Communicate((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Countdown); // 3 elements
        Wait(4, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        If(Entity Exists((Event Player).monkeyBomb)); // 4 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Doomfist Meteor Strike Impact Effect, Color(White), Position Of((Event Player).monkeyBomb), 1); // 8 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombTargets, Players Within Radius(Position Of((Event Player).monkeyBomb), 4, Team 2, Surfaces And All Barriers)); // 10 elements
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 22, 0); // 3 elements
            While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 22), <, Count Of((Event Player).monkeyBombTargets))); // 12 elements
                Damage(Value In Array((Event Player).monkeyBombTargets, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 22)), Null, If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array((Event Player).monkeyBombTargets, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 22)), Position Of((Event Player).monkeyBomb)), <=, 2), 150, 50)); // 33 elements
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 22, Add, 1); // 3 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Communicate((Event Player).monkeyBomb, Goodbye); // 3 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Destroy Effect(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 23)); // 6 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 23, Null); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, p_T, False); // 1 element
        Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        If(Compare(Players In Slot((Event Player).monkeyBombSlot, Team 1), ==, (Event Player).monkeyBomb)); // 11 elements
            Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 1, (Event Player).monkeyBombSlot); // 4 elements
        End; // 1 element

            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, monkeyBombActive, False); // 1 element
    }
}

//75 elements
rule ("352 FOLLOWERS attack nearest zombie") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Slot Of(Event Player) >= 4; // 3 elements
        Slot Of(Event Player) <= 7; // 3 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).isMonkeyBomb != True; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, False); // 1 element
        While(True); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), And(And(Has Spawned(Current Array Element), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 15)), Is In Line of Sight(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(Current Array Element), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element)))); // 26 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).followerTarget, !=, Null)); // 6 elements
                If(Not((Event Player).followerFiring)); // 4 elements
                    Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).followerTarget)), 720, To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 8 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, True); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)); // 2 elements
            Else If((Event Player).followerFiring); // 3 elements
                Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)); // 2 elements
                Stop Facing(Event Player); // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, followerFiring, False); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(0.25, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

    conditions {
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements

    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, papAppliedTier, If-Then-Else(Array Contains((Event Player).endingHeroes, Hero Of(Event Player)), 3, Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 5))); // 15 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, damagevariable, Value In Array(Array(100, 250, 500, 1000), (Event Player).papAppliedTier)); // 14 elements
        Set Damage Dealt(Event Player, (Event Player).damagevariable); // 3 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Packapunch, (Event Player).papAppliedTier); // 3 elements
    }
}

//147 elements
rule ("switch between 2 heros (needs ultimates, followers, ammo, systems working)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Crouch)) == True; // 4 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 200); // 5 elements
        Remove All Health Pools From Player(Event Player); // 1 element
        If(Compare((Event Player).currentswap, ==, False)); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, swapvar, Array(1, 0, 0, 1, 2)); // 12 elements
            Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(Subheroswap); // 1 element
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).currentswap, ==, 1)); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, swapvar, Array(0, 1, 3, 4, 5)); // 12 elements
            Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(Subheroswap); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player).heroswap, (Event Player).currentswap)); // 7 elements
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Set Player Health(Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 40), First Of((Event Player).swap))); // 11 elements
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 1), Value In Array((Event Player).abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swap, 2))); // 11 elements
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Ability 2), Value In Array((Event Player).abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swap, 3))); // 11 elements
        Set Ability Cooldown(Event Player, Button(Secondary Fire), Value In Array((Event Player).abilitycooldown, Value In Array((Event Player).swap, 4))); // 11 elements
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 44)); // 6 elements
        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Attach Players(Players In Slot(Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1), Event Player, Vector(-0.5, 1.8, -0.2)); // 14 elements
        Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1); // 5 elements
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
    }
}

//168 elements
rule ("202 Purchase Follower action") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).monkeyBombActive != True; // 3 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Reload)) == True; // 4 elements
        Is True For Any(Global.buyheros, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)) == True; // 10 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(1, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Global.hero), 1); // 5 elements
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.i)), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.i), !=, Null))); // 20 elements
                If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, Global.i), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest))); // 29 elements
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 45, Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.i)); // 6 elements
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, Global.i), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest)); // 20 elements
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points2, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, Global.i), 3), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), To Nearest)); // 20 elements
                    Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1); // 2 elements
                    Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins); // 1 element
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                    Call Subroutine(Subfollowerbuy); // 1 element
                    If(Not((Event Player).firstFollowerShown)); // 4 elements
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, firstFollowerShown, True); // 1 element
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                        Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up); // 1 element
                        Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote); // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                Else; // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global.i); // 2 elements
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).messageSpot)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 65), (Event Player).messageSpot))); // 19 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Break; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

    }
    actions {
        Wait(1, Abort When False); // 2 elements

        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements

    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        For Global Variable(i, 0, 6, 1); // 4 elements
            If(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Global.i)), <, 2)); // 11 elements
                If(Compare(Global.poweron, ==, 1)); // 6 elements
                    If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, Global.i), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest))); // 26 elements
                        If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).perk, Global.i), <, 3)); // 10 elements
                            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, Global.i), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest)); // 21 elements

                            Set Player Variable(Event Player, messageSpot, Global.i); // 2 elements
                            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.i, ==, 5), 5, 3)); // 11 elements
                            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins); // 1 element
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 121), (Event Player).messageSpot))); // 15 elements
                            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Ring Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70); // 5 elements
                            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                            Communicate(Event Player, Emote Up); // 1 element
                            Call Subroutine(PurchaseFreezeAfterEmote); // 1 element
                            Break; // 1 element
                        Else; // 1 element
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You already have the max tier!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 7 elements
                            Break; // 1 element
                        End; // 1 element
                    Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, <, Value In Array(Global.perkcosts, Global.i))); // 10 elements
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No points? NO perks!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 7 elements
                        Break; // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                Else; // 1 element
                    Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} The power is not ON!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 7 elements
                    Break; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("202 Purchase hero actions") {

        Team 1;
        All;
    }

        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements

    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        For Global Variable(idx, 0, Count Of(Global.buyheros), 1); // 5 elements
            If(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.idx)), <, 2), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.idx), !=, Null))); // 20 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, buySpot, Global.idx); // 2 elements
                If(Compare(Hero Of(Event Player), ==, Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot))); // 10 elements
                    If(And(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), 2), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest)), Compare((Event Player).currentmagazines, <, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10)))); // 40 elements
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), 2), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest)); // 18 elements
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Destination and Rate); // 14 elements
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10)); // 11 elements
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Magazines refilled!", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)))); // 11 elements
                        Break; // 1 element
                    Else; // 1 element
                        If(Compare((Event Player).currentmagazines, >=, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10))); // 16 elements
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("You already have full magazines!")); // 5 elements
                        Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, <, Divide(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), 2))); // 14 elements
                            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} No points? NO magazines!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 7 elements
                        End; // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                Else; // 1 element
                    If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest))); // 27 elements
                        Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)); // 6 elements
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, 0); // 3 elements
                        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroswap, (Event Player).currentswap, Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)); // 8 elements
                        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, (Event Player).currentswap, Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot))); // 9 elements
                        If(Compare(Server Load, <, 235)); // 5 elements
                            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70); // 5 elements
                            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1); // 5 elements
                        End; // 1 element
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest)); // 22 elements
                        Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1); // 2 elements
                        Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins); // 1 element
                        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), Value In Array(Global.herobuycosts, (Event Player).buySpot), Destination and Rate); // 14 elements
                        Attach Players(Players In Slot(Add(Slot Of(Event Player), 4), Team 1), Event Player, Vector(-0.5, 1.8, -0.2)); // 14 elements
                        If(Not(Array Contains((Event Player).allowedheroes, Hero Of(Event Player)))); // 7 elements
                            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes, Append To Array, Hero Of(Event Player)); // 2 elements
                        End; // 1 element
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, currentmagazines, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.Difficulty, ==, 5), 6, 10)); // 11 elements
                    Else; // 1 element
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} {1}", Hero Icon String(Value In Array(Global.hero, (Event Player).buySpot)), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 64), (Event Player).buySpot))); // 19 elements

                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }

    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).points, >, Subtract(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), (Event Player).creditcardlimit))); // 26 elements

                Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 1.5); // 6 elements
                Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70); // 5 elements

            Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 1.5); // 6 elements
            Start Forcing Player To Be Hero(Event Player, First Of(Random Value In Array(Randomized Array(All Heroes)))); // 4 elements
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, perk, 5, 0); // 3 elements
            Wait(0.1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            Resurrect(Event Player); // 1 element

                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Moira Fade Disappear Effect, Color(White), Event Player, 1); // 5 elements
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Buff Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70); // 5 elements
            End; // 1 element

                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, allowedheroes, Append To Array, Hero Of(Event Player)); // 2 elements

            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, Add, 1); // 3 elements
            Wait(1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements

            Set Global Variable(rngbox, Random Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations)); // 3 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 34, False); // 2 elements
            //lbl_0:
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, <, Subtract(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), 10, 950), Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), (Event Player).creditcardlimit))); // 26 elements

    }

        Set Player Variable(Event Player, coinPrice, If-Then-Else(Or(Or(Is True For Any(Global.buyheros, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)), Is True For Any(Global.bossbuypositions, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2))), And(And(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1), Compare(Global.rngbox, !=, Null)), Or(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Global.rngbox), <=, 2), And(Compare(Global.fireSale, ==, True), Is True For Any(Global.fireSaleBoxes, Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <=, 2)))))), 1, If-Then-Else(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 5)), <, 2), 5, If-Then-Else(Is True For Any(Array Slice(Global.buyperks, 0, 5), Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element), <, 2)), 3, 0)))); // 97 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).coinPrice, ==, 0)); // 7 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).coinUses, >=, 3)); // 7 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Already using 3 Ramattra coins here (an eighth of the price)")); // 5 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).ramattraCoins, >=, Multiply(Add((Event Player).coinUses, 1), (Event Player).coinPrice))); // 15 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, coinUses, Add, 1); // 2 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} Ramattra coins used: {1}. Price x {2}. Buy as usual", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)), (Event Player).coinUses, Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses))); // 14 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Not enough Ramattra coins: this costs {0} a use (you have {1})", (Event Player).coinPrice, (Event Player).ramattraCoins)); // 9 elements
    }
}

    }
    actions {

        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, heroiconswap, 0, Hero Icon String(Hero(Soldier: 76))); // 4 elements
        Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position(First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 2), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element))), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element))))), 800, To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 17 elements
        Start Transforming Throttle(Event Player, 1, 1, Vector(-0.109, Empty Array, 0.994)); // 8 elements
        While(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1)); // 6 elements
            If(Is Alive(Event Player)); // 2 elements
                Start Camera(Event Player, Subtract(Ray Cast Hit Position(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(0.778, 14.866, -7.117)), Null, Event Player, False), Vector(0.024, 0.451, -0.216)), Eye Position(Event Player), 20); // 26 elements

                    Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, Add(Ultimate Charge Percent(Event Player), 40)); // 5 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Stop Facing(Event Player); // 1 element
        Stop Transforming Throttle(Event Player); // 1 element
    }
}

//163 elements
rule ("137  get out of spawn bots") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches) > 0; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Status(Event Player, Null, Rooted, 9999); // 2 elements
        Wait(Add(0.25, Multiply(Slot Of(Event Player), 0.05)), Abort When False); // 8 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, breachPick, Random Integer(0, Subtract(Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), 1))); // 9 elements
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), >, 0)); // 10 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick, Max(0, Subtract(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 103)))); // 17 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, index, (Event Player).breachPick); // 3 elements
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index), <=, 0)); // 10 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, (Event Player).index, Color(Black)); // 4 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Global Variable At Index(color, (Event Player).index, Custom Color(Multiply(255, Subtract(1, Divide(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index), 100))), Divide(Multiply(255, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).index)), 100), 0, 255)); // 34 elements
            End; // 1 element
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, (Event Player).breachPick), <=, 0)); // 10 elements
                Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Orange), Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, (Event Player).breachPick), 3); // 10 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(1.5, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Start Forcing Player Outlines(Event Player, All Players(Team 1), True, Custom Color(Random Integer(0, 255), Random Integer(0, 255), Random Integer(0, 255), 255), Default); // 20 elements
            Clear Status(Event Player, Rooted); // 1 element
            Teleport(Event Player, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, (Event Player).breachPick)); // 6 elements
            Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("get nearest nodes for spawning") {

        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 103, 25); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 104, 8); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 105, 35); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 109, 15); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 91, 8); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable(teamPlayers, Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), And(Compare((Current Array Element).outofpsawn, ==, 1), Not(Is In Spawn Room(Current Array Element))))); // 15 elements
        If(And(Compare(Count Of(Global.teamPlayers), >, 0), Compare(Count Of(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes), >, 0))); // 15 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Vector(0, 0, 0)); // 8 elements
            For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global.teamPlayers), 1); // 5 elements
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Add, Position Of(Value In Array(Global.teamPlayers, Global.botspawnindex))); // 7 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 106, Divide, Count Of(Global.teamPlayers)); // 4 elements
            If(Or(Or(Compare(Global.averagePosition, ==, Null), Compare(Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), ==, 0)), Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 106), Global.averagePosition), >, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 109)))); // 29 elements
                Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 106)); // 5 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Empty Array); // 2 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Empty Array); // 2 elements
                For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Count Of(Global.globalActiveBreaches), 1); // 5 elements
                    If(Compare(Distance Between(Global.averagePosition, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Global.botspawnindex)), <, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 105))); // 15 elements
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreaches, Global.botspawnindex)); // 6 elements
                        Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.globalActiveBreachHealth, Global.botspawnindex)); // 6 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(candidates, Filtered Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Compare(Distance Between(Global.averagePosition, Current Array Element), <, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 105)))); // 14 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(candidates, Filtered Array(Global.candidates, And(Compare(Distance Between(Current Array Element, Closest Player To(Current Array Element, Team 1)), >=, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 104)), Not(Array Contains(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 107), Current Array Element))))); // 25 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 92, Min(Count Of(Global.candidates), Subtract(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 91), Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 107))))); // 17 elements
                For Global Variable(botspawnindex, 0, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 92), 1); // 7 elements
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 93, Random Value In Array(Global.candidates)); // 4 elements
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 107, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 93)); // 6 elements
                    Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 108, Append To Array, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, >, 5), 0, 100)); // 12 elements
                    Modify Global Variable(candidates, Remove From Array By Value, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 93)); // 5 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 107)); // 5 elements
                Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 108)); // 5 elements
                Call Subroutine(RebuildBreachEffects); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(5, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

        End; // 1 element
    }

//462 elements
rule ("Zombie chase: straight at you, pathfinding only when stuck or you are above") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Is In Spawn Room(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
        And(Array Contains(Global.bossBots, Event Player), Compare(Event Player, !=, Global.bossBody)) == False; // 10 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Compare((Current Array Element).p_T, ==, 0)), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element)))); // 16 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieTarget, First Of(Sorted Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element)))); // 8 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 10, If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, ==, Null), 9999, Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).zombieTarget))); // 15 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).zombieTarget, ==, Null)); // 6 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null); // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 0); // 2 elements
            Stop Throttle In Direction(Event Player); // 1 element
        Else; // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieFlatDistance, Distance Between(Multiply(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), Multiply(Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)))); // 21 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieHeightDifference, Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget)), Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)))); // 9 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).zombieMode, ==, 1)); // 7 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 9, And(Compare(Absolute Value((Event Player).zombieHeightDifference), <, 1.2), Or(Compare((Event Player).zombieFlatDistance, <, 3), And(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).zombieModeSince), >, 3), Is In Line of Sight(Add(Position Of(Event Player), Up), Add(Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget), Up), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))))); // 40 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 8, And(And(And(And(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 20), Global.pfMapReady), Not(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 9))), Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).zombieModeSince), <, 20)), Compare(Server Load, <, Add(Global.zombieLoadLimit, 60)))); // 36 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                If(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(Compare(Horizontal Speed Of(Event Player), <, 1.5), Compare((Event Player).zombieFlatDistance, >, 3)), Not((Event Player).zombieBusy)), Is On Ground(Event Player)), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Frozen))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Stunned))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Asleep))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Hacked)))); // 47 elements
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, Add, 1); // 2 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, 0); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
                If(Compare(Absolute Value((Event Player).zombieHeightDifference), >, 1.8)); // 8 elements
                    Modify Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, Add, 1); // 2 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, 0); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 8, And(And(And(And(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 20), Global.pfMapReady), Global.zombieExtrasAllowed), Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).zombieModeSince), >, 1.5)), Or(Compare((Event Player).zombieStuckChecks, >=, 3), Compare((Event Player).zombieAboveChecks, >=, 2)))); // 38 elements
            End; // 1 element
            If(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 8)); // 6 elements
                If(Compare((Event Player).zombieMode, !=, 1)); // 7 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 1); // 2 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince, Total Time Elapsed); // 1 element
                    Call Subroutine(pfLookAtDestination); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget)); // 4 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                If(Compare((Event Player).zombieMode, !=, 2)); // 7 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieMode, 2); // 2 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieModeSince, Total Time Elapsed); // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieStuckChecks, 0); // 2 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAboveChecks, 0); // 2 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfDestination, Null); // 1 element
                    Start Facing(Event Player, Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Eye Position((Event Player).zombieTarget)), 400, To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 8 elements
                End; // 1 element
                If(Not((Event Player).pfIsPathfinding)); // 4 elements
                    Start Throttle In Direction(Event Player, Direction Towards(Position Of(Event Player), Position Of((Event Player).zombieTarget)), 1, To World, Replace existing throttle, Direction and Magnitude); // 8 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost, If-Then-Else(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 21), Add(1, Min(Multiply(Max(Subtract((Event Player).zombieFlatDistance, 15), 0), 0.04), 0.6)), 1)); // 26 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieSpeedBoost, Divide(Round To Integer(Multiply((Event Player).zombieSpeedBoost, 10), Down), 10)); // 11 elements
            If(And(Not((Event Player).zombieBusy), Compare(Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <=, 6), Add(80, Multiply(8, Global.roundnumber)), 128), (Event Player).zombieSpeedBoost), !=, (Event Player).zombieAppliedSpeed))); // 31 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, zombieAppliedSpeed, Multiply(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.roundnumber, <=, 6), Add(80, Multiply(8, Global.roundnumber)), 128), (Event Player).zombieSpeedBoost)); // 21 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 4, Divide((Event Player).zombieAppliedSpeed, 100)); // 7 elements
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, (Event Player).zombieAppliedSpeed); // 3 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(If-Then-Else(Global.zombieExtrasAllowed, 0.25, 0.5), Abort When False); // 7 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

    actions {
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, False); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(pfcompressionTable, Custom String(" {0}", Global.decodeTable)); // 6 elements
        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors, Empty Array); // 1 element
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(First Of(Global.pfDecompress)), 1); // 6 elements
            For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1)), 6); // 9 elements
                Modify Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_nodes, Append To Array, Vector(Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Global.pfI2)), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 1)))), 524288), 100), Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 2))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 3)))), 524288), 100), Divide(Subtract(Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 4))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(First Of(Global.pfDecompress), Global.pfI1), Add(Global.pfI2, 5)))), 524288), 100))); // 118 elements
                If(Not(Modulo(Global.pfI2, 24))); // 6 elements
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 0); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Empty Array); // 1 element
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 1)), 1); // 8 elements
            For Global Variable(pfI2, 0, String Length(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 1), Global.pfI1)), 1); // 11 elements
                Set Global Variable(pfnext, Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 1), Global.pfI1), Global.pfI2))); // 14 elements
                If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 17), ==, 0)); // 9 elements
                    If(Not(Global.pfnext)); // 3 elements
                        Modify Global Variable(pfLoadedMap_neighbors, Append To Array, Array(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors)); // 4 elements
                        Set Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Empty Array); // 1 element
                    Else; // 1 element
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 1); // 3 elements
                        Modify Global Variable(pfcurrentNeighbors, Append To Array, Array(Array(Subtract(Global.pfnext, 1), Empty Array))); // 10 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                Else If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 17), ==, 1)); // 9 elements
                    If(Not(Global.pfnext)); // 3 elements
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 17, 0); // 3 elements
                    Else; // 1 element
                        Set Global Variable At Index(pfcurrentNeighbors, Subtract(Count Of(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors), 1), Array(First Of(Last Of(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors)), Append To Array(Value In Array(Last Of(Global.pfcurrentNeighbors), 1), Subtract(Global.pfnext, 1)))); // 23 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                If(Not(Modulo(Global.pfI2, 20))); // 6 elements
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(pfNodeComponent, Empty Array); // 1 element
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Count Of(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes), 1); // 5 elements
            Modify Global Variable(pfNodeComponent, Append To Array, Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 3), Round To Integer(Divide(Global.pfI1, 42), Down)), Add(Multiply(Modulo(Global.pfI1, 42), 3), 2)))); // 28 elements
            If(Not(Modulo(Global.pfI1, 20))); // 6 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 18, Mapped Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, Current Array Index)); // 5 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 19, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        For Global Variable(pfI1, 0, Add(Last Of(Sorted Array(Global.pfNodeComponent, Current Array Element)), 1), 1); // 10 elements
            Set Global Variable(_arrayConstructor, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 19)); // 5 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(_arrayConstructor, Global.pfI1, Filtered Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 18), Compare(Value In Array(Global.pfNodeComponent, Current Array Element), ==, Global.pfI1))); // 15 elements
            Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 19, Global._arrayConstructor); // 3 elements
            Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, True); // 1 element
    }
}

//126 elements
rule ("【PF Core Subroutine】☞ Look up next hop (Floyd-Warshall, RLE base 1024)") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        pfLookupNextHop;
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopPosition, Add(Multiply(Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 3), Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), Down)), Multiply(Modulo((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), 3))), 1024), Index Of String Char(Global.pfcompressionTable, Char In String(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfDecompress, 3), Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), Down)), Add(Multiply(Modulo((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 42), 3), 1))))); // 61 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 5, (Event Player).pfGoalNode); // 4 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 6, 0); // 3 elements
        Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar); // 1 element
        While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 5), >, Modulo((Event Player).pfHopChar, 128))); // 14 elements
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 5, Subtract, Add(Modulo((Event Player).pfHopChar, 128), 1)); // 10 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, pfHopPosition, Add, 1); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(pfReadNextHopChar); // 1 element
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 6, Add, 1); // 3 elements
            If(Compare(Modulo(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 6), 25), ==, 0)); // 13 elements
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 7, Round To Integer(Divide((Event Player).pfHopChar, 128), Down)); // 9 elements
    }
}

                Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0}: no path from node {1} to node {2}.", Event Player, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, (Event Player).pfGoalNode)); // 9 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 2, Empty Array); // 2 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, -1); // 2 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfPreviousNode, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex); // 3 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfCurrentNodeIndex, First Of(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_neighbors, (Event Player).pfPreviousNode), Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 7)))); // 14 elements

            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, If-Then-Else(Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1), (Event Player).pfDestination, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex))); // 17 elements
        Call Subroutine(pfUpdateNodeMeta); // 1 element

}

//138 elements
rule ("【PF Core】Watch progress (checked every 4 ticks)") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).pfIsPathfinding == True; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, False); // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, 0); // 2 elements
        While((Event Player).pfIsPathfinding); // 3 elements
            If(And(And(And((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, !=, -1)), Compare(Distance Between(Multiply(Position Of(Event Player), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), Multiply((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, Vector(1, Empty Array, 1))), <=, Multiply(0.6, Max(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 4), 1)))), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player)), Y Component Of((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition))), <=, 1.5))); // 60 elements
                Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer); // 1 element
            Else If(And((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, Compare(Distance Between(Divide(Add((Event Player).pfWalkingFromPosition, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), 2), Event Player), >, Multiply(Add(Distance Between((Event Player).pfWalkingFromPosition, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), 4), 1.2)))); // 31 elements
                Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            If(Or(Not((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), Compare(Magnitude Of(Velocity Of(Event Player)), >=, 3))); // 12 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, 0); // 2 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, False); // 1 element
            Else If(Not((Event Player).pfSlowSince)); // 4 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfSlowSince, Total Time Elapsed); // 1 element
            Else If(Compare(Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, (Event Player).pfSlowSince), >, 2.1)); // 9 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfIsStuck, True); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(0.064, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//218 elements
rule ("【PF Core】Insurance") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        All;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).pfIsPathfinding == True; // 3 elements
        (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition == True; // 3 elements

    actions {

            Call Subroutine(pfProgressEventPlayer); // 1 element
        Else If(And(And(And(Compare((Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition, !=, (Event Player).pfDestination), Compare((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, ==, -1)), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).pfSavedTarget, (Event Player).pfDestination), <, 5)), Is In Line of Sight(Add((Event Player).pfSavedTarget, Up), Add((Event Player).pfDestination, Up), Barriers Do Not Block LOS))); // 41 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, pfWalkingToPosition, (Event Player).pfDestination); // 3 elements
        Else If(And(And(And(And(And(And(And(Compare(Distance Between(Event Player, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), >, 0.4), Not(Or(Not((Event Player).pfSavedTarget), Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).pfDestination, (Event Player).pfSavedTarget), >, 5)))), Or((Event Player).pfIsStuck, Compare(Subtract(Distance Between((Event Player).pfWalkingFromPosition, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition), Multiply(4.95, Subtract(Total Time Elapsed, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 1)))), <, 0))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Rooted))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Frozen))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Asleep))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Knocked Down))), Not(Has Status(Event Player, Stunned)))); // 80 elements
            If(Compare(Subtract(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 3), 2), ==, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex)); // 14 elements
                Log To Inspector(Custom String("Pathfinder {0} struggled to reach node {1} at {2}. Try adjusting your map in the editor.", Event Player, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, Value In Array(Global.pfLoadedMap_nodes, (Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex))); // 12 elements
                Teleport(Event Player, (Event Player).pfWalkingToPosition); // 3 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 3, Add((Event Player).pfCurrentNodeIndex, 2)); // 7 elements
                Call Subroutine(pfRecalibrate); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(2, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}
