//878 elements
rule ("Collision Logic") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        Global.start == 1; // 3 elements
        Is Dummy Bot(Event Player) == False; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 46), ==, 1)); // 9 elements
            Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 46), ==, 0), 999999995904); // 10 elements
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        If(Compare((Event Player).outofpsawn, ==, 0)); // 7 elements
            Wait Until(Compare((Event Player).outofpsawn, ==, 1), 999999995904); // 8 elements
            Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Divide(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Position Of(Event Player)), 2)); // 8 elements
        Wait(Add(Add(0.16, Divide(Multiply(0.32, Server Load Peak), 255)), Multiply(Slot Of(Event Player), 0.016)), Ignore Condition); // 16 elements
        Wait Until(Compare(Max(Server Load Average, Server Load), <, 245), 0.5); // 8 elements
        If(Or(Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).lastsavedpos, Event Player), >, 40), Compare((Event Player).doorSkipCheck, ==, True))); // 16 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorSkipCheck, False); // 1 element
            Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
            Abort; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestwall, Filtered Array(Mapped Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Current Array Index), Or(Or(Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), Event Player), <=, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Current Array Element))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).active_wall, Current Array Element), ==, 1)), Compare(Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), (Event Player).lastsavedpos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Current Array Element)), >, 0), !=, Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Current Array Element), Event Player), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Current Array Element)), >, 0))))); // 67 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 11, 0); // 3 elements
        While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 11), <, Count Of((Event Player).closestwall))); // 12 elements
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.z), ==, 0)); // 9 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, 0); // 3 elements
                Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(z, Value In Array((Event Player).closestwall, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 11))); // 10 elements
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.z), ==, 1)); // 9 elements
                If(And(Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), >=, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), <=, Y Component Of(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.2, Empty Array)))))); // 29 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)); // 5 elements
                Else If(And(Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), >=, Y Component Of(Position Of(Event Player))), Compare(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), <=, Y Component Of(Add(Eye Position(Event Player), Vector(Empty Array, 0.2, Empty Array)))))); // 29 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)); // 5 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, closestbodypos, Position Of(Event Player)); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, fullbodypos, Vector(X Component Of(Eye Position(Event Player)), Y Component Of((Event Player).closestbodypos), Z Component Of(Eye Position(Event Player)))); // 11 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, filterpos, Add((Event Player).fullbodypos, Divide(Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z), Dot Product(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z))), Dot Product(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z))))); // 37 elements
                If(Compare(Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).lastsavedpos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)), >, 0), !=, Compare(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)), >, 0))); // 40 elements
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 12, Divide(Dot Product(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)), Dot Product(Direction Towards((Event Player).lastsavedpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Facing, Global.z)))); // 30 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, prevpos_intersection, Add((Event Player).fullbodypos, Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).lastsavedpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), Vector(1, 1, 1)), Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 12)))); // 26 elements
                    If(And(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Dot Product(Subtract((Event Player).prevpos_intersection, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.z)), Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2))), <=, Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2)), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of((Event Player).prevpos_intersection), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z)))), <=, Divide(Subtract(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z))), 2)))); // 66 elements
                        Cancel Primary Action(Event Player); // 1 element
                        Teleport(Event Player, Add((Event Player).prevpos_intersection, Multiply(Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).prevpos_intersection, (Event Player).lastsavedpos), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), 2))); // 21 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
                If(And(Compare(Distance Between((Event Player).fullbodypos, (Event Player).filterpos), <=, 4), And(Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Dot Product(Subtract((Event Player).filterpos, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z)), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.z)), Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2))), <=, Divide(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Width, Global.z), 2)), Compare(Absolute Value(Subtract(Y Component Of((Event Player).filterpos), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z)))), <=, Divide(Subtract(Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.z)), Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.z))), 2))))); // 78 elements
                    If(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player).active_wall, Global.z), ==, False)); // 9 elements
                        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, 1); // 3 elements
                        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 1), ==, False)); // 8 elements
                            Set Gravity(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
                        End; // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                    Apply Impulse(Event Player, Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).filterpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1)), 1, To World, Cancel Contrary Motion); // 15 elements
                    If(Compare((Event Player).doorNotif, ==, 0)); // 7 elements
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, 1); // 2 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                    If(And(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)), Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global.costmultiplier), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 3))), (Event Player).creditcardlimit)))); // 37 elements
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Good Explosion, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), 50); // 9 elements
                        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Lúcio Sound Barrier Cast Sound, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Center, Global.z), 100); // 9 elements
                        Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(1000, Global.costmultiplier), Value In Array(Array(1, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3), Value In Array((Event Player).perk, 3))), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest)); // 29 elements
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 80, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Zone, Global.z)); // 6 elements
                        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 79, If-Then-Else(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 80), ==, -1), Array(Global.z), Filtered Array(Mapped Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Current Array Index), Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Zone, Current Array Element), ==, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 80))))); // 31 elements
                        For Global Variable(i, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 79)), 1); // 8 elements
                            Set Global Variable(z, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 79), Global.i)); // 8 elements
                            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_State, Global.z), ==, 1)); // 9 elements
                                Set Global Variable(doorIndex, Global.z); // 2 elements
                                Call Subroutine(DestroyDoorEffects); // 1 element
                                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_State, Global.z, 0); // 3 elements
                                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, 0); // 3 elements
                                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 56, Append To Array, Add(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 3), Global.z)); // 9 elements
                            End; // 1 element
                        End; // 1 element
                        Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("Zone bought!")); // 5 elements
                        Set Player Variable(Event Player, doorNotif, 0); // 2 elements
                        Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Multiply(Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), (Event Player).H), 1000, Destination and Rate); // 10 elements
                        Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                        Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
                    End; // 1 element
                    Set Move Speed(Event Player, Subtract(100, Multiply(Dot Product(Direction Towards(Eye Position(Event Player), Add(Eye Position(Event Player), World Vector Of(Throttle Of(Event Player), Event Player, Rotation))), Multiply(Direction Towards((Event Player).filterpos, (Event Player).fullbodypos), -1)), 100))); // 28 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, active_wall, Global.z, 0); // 3 elements
                    Set Move Speed(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 11, Add, 1); // 3 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

                Create Beam Effect(All Players(Team 1), Junkrat Trap Chain Beam, Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamSpan, Global.dooreffectsequencer)), Color(Red), Visible To); // 18 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamA, Global.dooreffectsequencer, Last Created Entity); // 2 elements
                Create Beam Effect(All Players(Team 1), Junkrat Trap Chain Beam, Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Vector(Empty Array, Y Component Of(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamSpan, Global.dooreffectsequencer)), Empty Array)), Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_BeamSpan, Global.dooreffectsequencer), Vector(1, Empty Array, 1))), Color(Red), Visible To); // 35 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_BeamB, Global.dooreffectsequencer, Last Created Entity); // 2 elements
                If(Not(Modulo(Global.dooreffectsequencer, 3))); // 6 elements
                    Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
                End; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element

        End; // 1 element
    }

//239 elements
rule ("488 button held teleporter") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 1;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Interact)) == True; // 4 elements
        Count Of(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 3, Team 1, Off)) > 0; // 13 elements
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare(Count Of(Filtered Array(All Living Players(Team 1), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element)))), ==, Count Of(Filtered Array(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 3, Team 1, Off), And(Is Alive(Current Array Element), Not(Is Dummy Bot(Current Array Element))))))); // 29 elements
            If(Compare(Global.teleporterwait, ==, False)); // 5 elements
                Set Global Variable(teleporterwait, True); // 1 element
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Empty Array); // 1 element
                Create Effect(Event Player, Ana Nano Boosted Effect, Team Of(Event Player), Event Player, 1, Visible To Position and Radius); // 3 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                Create Effect(Local Player, Winston Tesla Cannon Target Effect, Color(Black), Event Player, 80, Visible To Position and Radius); // 3 elements
                Modify Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Set Status(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 5, Team 2, Off), Event Player, Stunned, 0.25); // 12 elements
                Destroy Effect(First Of((Event Player).teleporterEffects)); // 4 elements
                Play Effect(Local Player, Ring Explosion Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 80); // 3 elements
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Kill(Players Within Radius(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), 5, Team 2, Off), First Of(All Living Players(Team 1))); // 14 elements
                Destroy Effect(Value In Array((Event Player).teleporterEffects, 1)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, teleporterEffects, Empty Array); // 1 element
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 120, Color(Red)); // 3 elements
                If(Compare(Count Of(Global.teleporterpositions), >, 0)); // 7 elements
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 136, False); // 2 elements
                    Set Global Variable(telespot, Modulo(Add(Global.telespot, 1), Count Of(Global.teleporterpositions))); // 9 elements
                    Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                    Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, 1); // 3 elements
                    Set Player Variable(Event Player, lastsavedpos, Null); // 1 element
                    Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                    Wait Until(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 136), ==, True), 15); // 9 elements
                    Teleport(All Players(Team 1), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot)); // 7 elements
                    For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 4, 1); // 4 elements
                        If(And(Is Dead(Players In Slot(Global.forindex, Team 1)), Compare(Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.Died, Global.forindex), Vector(500, Empty Array, 500)), >, 1))); // 23 elements
                            Set Global Variable At Index(Died, Global.forindex, Nearest Walkable Position(Add(Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Vector(Subtract(Multiply(Global.forindex, 1.5), 2.25), Empty Array, 2)))); // 20 elements
                        End; // 1 element
                    End; // 1 element
                    Skip(Value In Array(Array(5, 0, 2, 4), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(0, 1, 2), Global.telespot), 1))); // 25 elements
                    Call Subroutine(zone1); // 1 element
                    Skip(3); // 2 elements
                    Call Subroutine(zone2); // 1 element
                    Skip(1); // 2 elements
                    Call Subroutine(zone3); // 1 element
                    //lbl_0:
                    //lbl_1:
                End; // 1 element
                Wait(0.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 46, 0); // 3 elements
                Set Global Variable(doorsNeedDrawing, True); // 1 element
    }
}

//66 elements
rule ("wave boss dispatch") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        SmDispatch;
    }
    actions {
        If(Compare((Event Player).smCode, >=, 11)); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, (Event Player).smCode); // 3 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).smCode, >=, 6)); // 7 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).smHolding, ==, False)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 31, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 111), Subtract((Event Player).smCode, 6))); // 13 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 32, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 30)); // 7 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, smHolding, True); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).smCode, >=, 1)); // 7 elements
            Press Button(Event Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 111), Subtract((Event Player).smCode, 1))); // 12 elements
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

//44 elements
rule ("wave boss: find my row") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Has Spawned(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smRow, If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Global.bossBots, Event Player), 0, Add(Index Of Array Value(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 110), Hero Of(Event Player)), 1))); // 18 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).smRow, >, 0)); // 7 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 113), Subtract((Event Player).smRow, 1))); // 12 elements
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, 0); // 3 elements
            Call Subroutine(SmDispatch); // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("wave boss: timed sequence") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
        (Event Player).smRow > 0; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 28, 0); // 3 elements
        While(Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 28), <, 6)); // 10 elements
            Set Player Variable(Event Player, smEntry, Multiply(Add(Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, 1), 6), Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 28)), 5)); // 19 elements
            Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 29, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 10)); // 7 elements
            If(And(And(Compare(Global.Difficulty, >=, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 4))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 29), >=, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 2)))), Compare(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 29), <, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 3))))); // 49 elements
                Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Global.smActions, (Event Player).smEntry)); // 6 elements
                Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Value In Array(Global.smActions, Add((Event Player).smEntry, 1))); // 10 elements
                Call Subroutine(SmDispatch); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Wait(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 114), Subtract(Global.Difficulty, 1)), Abort When False); // 11 elements
            Modify Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 28, Add, 1); // 3 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

//75 elements
rule ("wave boss: health tri­gger") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smRow > 0; // 4 elements
        Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, 1), 3)) > 0; // 16 elements
        Normalized Health(Event Player) <= Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, 1), 3)); // 16 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smCode, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Add(Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, 1), 3), 1))); // 18 elements
        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 30, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 112), Add(Multiply(Subtract((Event Player).smRow, 1), 3), 2))); // 19 elements
        Call Subroutine(SmDispatch); // 1 element
    }
}

//23 elements
rule ("wave boss: hold button") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smHolding == True; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Start Holding Button(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 31)); // 6 elements
        Wait(Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 32), Ignore Condition); // 6 elements
        Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 31)); // 6 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smHolding, False); // 1 element
    }
}

//20 elements
rule ("wave boss: special 11 ground slam") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smSpecial == 11; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Bad Explosion, Color(Orange), Event Player, 8); // 5 elements
        Damage(Players Within Radius(Event Player, 8, Team 1, Off), Event Player, 40); // 8 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, 0); // 2 elements
    }
}

//13 elements
rule ("wave boss: special 12 crouch with full ultimate") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        All;
    }
    conditions {
        (Event Player).smSpecial == 12; // 4 elements
    }
    actions {
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Crouch)); // 2 elements
        Set Ultimate Charge(Event Player, 100); // 2 elements
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, smSpecial, 0); // 2 elements
    }
}

//298 elements
rule ("build the zone's doors") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        BuildZoneDoors;
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 2, 0); // 3 elements
        For Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.allDoors_Corner), 1); // 5 elements
            Set Global Variable(doorIndex, Global.doorBuildIndex); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(DestroyDoorEffects); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(All Players(Team 1), active_wall, Empty Array); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 3, Value In Array(Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 71), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 73), Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 75)), Global.telespot)); // 21 elements
        Call Subroutine(LoadZoneDoorData); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, 0); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(looping, 0); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Corner, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_State, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Empty Array); // 1 element
        While(Compare(Global.doorBuildIndex, <, Count Of(Global.doorVectors))); // 7 elements
            If(Not(Modulo(Global.doorBuildIndex, 2))); // 6 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Corner, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Global.doorVectors, Global.doorBuildIndex))); // 7 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Append To Array, Array(Null)); // 3 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Center, Append To Array, Array(Null)); // 3 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Facing, Append To Array, Array(Direction From Angles(Add(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 2)), 180), 0))); // 16 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Side, Append To Array, Array(Direction From Angles(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 2)), 90), 0))); // 16 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Width, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Global.looping))); // 7 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_State, Append To Array, Array(If-Then-Else(Array Contains(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 56), Add(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 3), Global.doorBuildIndex)), 0, 1))); // 21 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_Zone, Append To Array, Array(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 57), Global.doorBuildIndex))); // 10 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamSpan, Append To Array, Array(Add(Multiply(Direction From Angles(Subtract(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 2)), 90), 0), Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Global.looping)), Multiply(Up, Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 1)))))); // 33 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamA, Append To Array, Array(Null)); // 3 elements
            Modify Global Variable(allDoors_BeamB, Append To Array, Array(Null)); // 3 elements
            If(Compare(Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 3)), ==, 1)); // 12 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_Width, Global.doorBuildIndex, Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 4))); // 9 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.doorBuildIndex, Add(Add(Value In Array(Global.doorVectors, Global.doorBuildIndex), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 4)))), Multiply(Up, Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Add(Global.looping, 5))))); // 32 elements
                Modify Global Variable(looping, Add, 6); // 2 elements
            Else; // 1 element
                Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.doorBuildIndex, Add(Add(Value In Array(Global.doorVectors, Global.doorBuildIndex), Multiply(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Side, Global.doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global.doorParams, Global.looping))), Vector(Empty Array, 50, Empty Array))); // 24 elements
                Modify Global Variable(looping, Add, 4); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable At Index(allDoors_Center, Global.doorBuildIndex, Divide(Add(Value In Array(Global.allDoors_Corner, Global.doorBuildIndex), Value In Array(Global.allDoors_OppositeCorner, Global.doorBuildIndex)), 2)); // 15 elements
            Modify Global Variable(doorBuildIndex, Add, 1); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(doorVectors, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(doorParams, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 57, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 2, 1); // 3 elements
    }
}

//514 elements
rule ("zone loading: set up the team's zone") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.mapDataLoaded == True; // 2 elements
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 134) != Global.telespot; // 6 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable(zoneLoading, True); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 134, Global.telespot); // 3 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 138, And(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 8), !=, Null), Is Alive(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 8)))); // 16 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 139, 0); // 3 elements
        While(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 139), <, 12)); // 9 elements
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 139), Team 2), !=, Null)); // 11 elements
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 139)); // 6 elements
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 139, Add, 1); // 3 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 136, True); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreaches, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(globalActiveBreachHealth, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(averagePosition, Null); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(RebuildBreachEffects); // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(BuildZoneDoors); // 1 element
        Wait(1.5, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        Wait Until(And(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 135), ==, False), Compare(Global.doorsNeedDrawing, ==, False)), 30); // 15 elements
        Set Global Variable(pfMapReady, False); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(pfDecompress, 0); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
        If(Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 134), ==, 0)); // 9 elements
            Set Global Variable(pfDecompress, Array(Array(Custom String("޴୐޼ૼ޵׋޳̶޼૷޴૱ް੟޼ૢ޵˷ްֻ޼ࡿ޶ُ޲̕޼ࢀ޶ા޳ֵ޼ࡿ޶¿޵̊޼ଁ޶̔޵׋޼૥޶ி޵੔޼૥޷޵ૈ޼૥޷࣎޳ெ޼ࢁ޶࢔޲૚޼ु޷ͤ޳̃޼ु޷ੇްً޼޳޷ؚް޼ޯ޸ް|޼ु޸॥ް޶޼ु޸੐ް؀޼਺޻҈޲̳޼৽޻௎޲৅޼਒޼ࣳ޳޼৽޻ோ"), Custom String("޲৉޼਒޻؎ް׏޼਻޼૝ޯ॥޼਺޼¤ޯ੅޼਺޻࡫ް˹޼ੇ޻ிްী޻௎޼޴̌޼:޼]޴̨޼:޻ؑ޵̘޼:޻؍޵̔޼:޼ࠟ޴͢޼:޼ࢀ޴͈޼͆޺ࢎ޵ࠦ޼ޯ޺؎޶׵޼ޯ޺ொ޶׽޼ޯ޺׆޶঳޼ޯ޺΋޶࣢޼ޯ޹ؘ޵ۘ޼ޯ޹֎޵؄޼ޯ޹੍޳֤޼ޯ޹਀޳҃޼ޯ޸܊"), Custom String("޲ީ޼ޯ޸਻޲࡫޼ޯ޹੶޲؎޼ّ޺ࢎ޲ۘ޼֣޻ׄްؒ޻௎޻ִް؋޻௎޻਒ް˕޼ੇ޼ްِ޻௎޼΃ް~޼਺޻୾޵߾޼ࡿ޸͓޶ֹ޼ु޸՝޶֓޼஽޹϶޴֠޼ࡿ޷ீ޲՘޼ޯ޹;޲΅޼৙޹ૉް࠳޼਻޹ூްި޼਻޾˷޲֣޼৙޾̖޲֓޼ޯ޾਒ް޾޼ु޿͏޲ַ޼ޯ޿΢"), Custom String("޳΢޼ޯ޿֞޳͍޼ޯ޾͊޲࡚޼ޯ޾؊޵׶޼ޯ޾̼޵ޫ޼ޯ޽ޮ޴֪޼͆޽ؚ޳޼ّ޽࠮޲ٔ޼ّ޽߱޲۠޼֣޼ंް݈޻்޼৅޳͗޼ّ޺ࡴ޶֨޼ࠩ޻஻޴ࣶ޼ࠫ޻௒޶֙޼ޯ޽޶۟޼ޯ޽ঁ޶ુ޼ޯ޾̩޶̷޼ޯ޾঴޵۠޼ޯ޾ু޵܇޼ࡿ޿े޶ֲ޼ु޿ऺ޶׭޼஽޾ૈ"), Custom String("޴˅޼ࡿ߫«޴̔޼ࢀ߬਑޲઱޼ु߫ॗ޳˰޼ु͔߫޵̡޼૿߬઱޴૓޼૷߭ે޳΅޼૵߮̔ް੣޼૞͎߮ްֽ޼ࡿ߬ࢅ޲޼ࢁ̢߬ްؐ޼ޯؙ߫ް ޼ޯ޿૳ް޼ु޿ͦ޳֠޼ࡿ߬ૅ޵֧޼૥߬±޵੥޼૥߫ୀ޵૗޼૥߫֬޶־޻ெ޼ࣣ޶ࣿ޻௕޼ ޷ͦ޻଩޼#޶׵޻ி޻ٗ"), Custom String("޶؅޼ ޺਑޷࠷޼˜޺ं޷܋޼֣޹઻޶৓޼ޯ޹ૈ")), Array(Custom String("# (  ! $ '  # %  $ &  % ' /  & # ( ,  ' ! )  ( *  ) +  *  ' -   , .  -  & :  / ;  : <  ;    > _   = ? //205 elements
rule ("zone loading: bring the zombies back") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 137) == True; // 5 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 137, False); // 2 elements
        If(And(And(Compare(Global.start, ==, 1), Compare(Global.numberOfZombies, >=, 1)), Compare(Global.Bosssequencer, ==, 0))); // 20 elements
            Call Subroutine(setbotamount); // 1 element
            Modify Global Variable(botamount, Min, Subtract(Global.numberOfZombies, If-Then-Else(And(Compare(Global.spawntype, !=, 4), Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 138), ==, True)), 1, 0))); // 23 elements
            If(And(And(Compare(Global.spawntype, !=, 4), Compare(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 138), ==, True)), Compare(Players In Slot(0, Team 2), ==, Null))); // 24 elements
                Create Dummy Bot(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 7), Global.g), Team 2, 0, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Vector(0, 0, 0)); // 19 elements
                Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Players In Slot(0, Team 2)); // 6 elements
                Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
            Set Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4), 0, 1)); // 11 elements
            While(And(Compare(Global.zoneRefillSlot, <=, If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4), Subtract(Global.botamount, 1), Global.botamount)), Compare(Global.zoneLoading, ==, False))); // 24 elements
                If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.zoneRefillSlot, Team 2), ==, Null)); // 8 elements
                    Create Dummy Bot(If-Then-Else(Compare(Global.spawntype, ==, 4), Value In Array(Global.hero, Random Integer(0, 37)), If-Then-Else(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneRefillSlot, 2), ==, 1), Value In Array(Global.bot1spawn, Global.g), Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 15), Global.g))), Team 2, Global.zoneRefillSlot, First Of(Spawn Points(Team 2)), Vector(0, 0, 0)); // 49 elements
                    Set Global Variable At Index(omnicspawn, Global.zoneRefillSlot, Global.zoneRefillSlot); // 3 elements
                    Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Modify Global Variable(zoneRefillSlot, Add, 1); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
    }
}

//174 elements
rule ("all the effects") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1; // 3 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(buyperks, 8, Vector(240.047, 46.147, 174.277)); // 8 elements
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 3, 1); // 4 elements
            Create Effect(Local Player, Good Aura, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 120), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 58), Global.forindex)), Visible To Position Radius and Color); // 18 elements
            Create Effect(Local Player, Sparkles, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 120), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 58), Global.forindex)), Visible To Position Radius and Color); // 18 elements
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Global.telespot), Evaluate Once(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 58), Global.forindex)), Visible To Position Radius and Color); // 15 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(1, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        For Global Variable(forindex, 0, 3, 1); // 4 elements
            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.Died, Evaluate Once(Global.forindex)), 4, Visible To Position and Radius); // 9 elements
            Create Effect(Local Player, Sphere, Color(White), Value In Array(Global.Died, Evaluate Once(Global.forindex)), (Players In Slot(Evaluate Once(Global.forindex), Team 1)).sea, Visible To Position and Radius); // 16 elements
            Create In-World Text(Local Player, Custom String("{0}", Evaluate Once(Random Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 61)))), Value In Array(Global.Died, Evaluate Once(Global.forindex)), 3, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility); // 20 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Purple), Global.rngbox, 2, Visible To Position and Radius); // 4 elements
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 10, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), 1, Visible To Position and Radius); // 10 elements
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 25, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), 1, Visible To Position and Radius); // 10 elements
        Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Sky Blue), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 40, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), 1, Visible To Position and Radius); // 10 elements
        Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Question Mark), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Global.rngbox), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(Purple), Default Visibility); // 11 elements
        Create Effect(Local Player, Sparkles, Color(Yellow), Global.rngbox, 2, Visible To Position and Radius); // 4 elements
        Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Symmetra Photon Projector, Null, Add(Up, Global.rngbox), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To); // 5 elements
    }
}

//78 elements
rule ("clear zone markers") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        ClearZoneMarkers;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.zoneEffects), 1); // 5 elements
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global.zoneEffects, Global.zoneMarkerIndex)); // 5 elements
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 10), ==, 9)); // 9 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.zoneTexts), 1); // 5 elements
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Global.zoneTexts, Global.zoneMarkerIndex)); // 5 elements
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 10), ==, 9)); // 9 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 129)), 1); // 8 elements
            Destroy Icon(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 129), Global.zoneMarkerIndex)); // 8 elements
            If(Compare(Modulo(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, 10), ==, 9)); // 9 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(zoneEffects, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(zoneTexts, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 129, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(powerconnected, Null); // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("draw zone markers") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        DrawZoneMarkers;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, 7, 1); // 4 elements
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot)); // 18 elements
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), 1, Visible To and Color); // 9 elements
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                If(Compare(Global.zoneMarkerIndex, !=, 6)); // 6 elements
                    Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To); // 8 elements
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                    Create In-World Text(Local Player, Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 143), Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex))), If-Then-Else(Compare(Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex), ==, 0), 3.25, 2.25), Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To Position and String, Color(White), Default Visibility); // 35 elements
                    Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 1 element
                Else; // 1 element
                    Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Zarya Graviton, Null, Add(Vector(Empty Array, 1.5, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex))), Up, 0.05, Visible To Friendly To); // 14 elements

                        Create Icon(All Living Players(Team 1), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 6)), Bolt, Visible To and Position, Color(Yellow), True); // 14 elements

            Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Red), Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 8), 1, Visible To); // 7 elements
            Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
            Create Projectile Effect(Local Player, Baptiste Biotic Launcher, Null, Value In Array(Global.buyperks, 8), Up, 0.1, Visible To Friendly To); // 6 elements

            Modify Global Variable(zoneTexts, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
        For Global Variable(zoneMarkerIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.buyheros), 1); // 5 elements
            If(And(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot), Compare(Value In Array(Global.hero, Global.zoneMarkerIndex), !=, Null))); // 27 elements
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Yellow), Value In Array(Global.buyheros, Evaluate Once(Global.zoneMarkerIndex)), 2, Visible To); // 9 elements
                Modify Global Variable(zoneEffects, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element

            End; // 1 element

rule ("fire sale boxes") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Global;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.start == 1; // 3 elements
        Global.fireSale == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 132, Global.telespot); // 3 elements
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.mysterylocations), 1); // 5 elements
            If(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot)); // 18 elements
                Modify Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Append To Array, Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex)); // 5 elements
            End; // 1 element
            If(And(Compare(Index Of Array Value(Global.teleporterpositions, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.teleporterpositions, Distance Between(Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex), Current Array Element)))), ==, Global.telespot), Compare(Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Global.fireSaleIndex), !=, Global.rngbox))); // 28 elements
                Create Effect(Local Player, Ring, Color(Purple), Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Evaluate Once(Global.fireSaleIndex)), 2, Visible To); // 9 elements
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 2 elements
                Create Effect(Local Player, Light Shaft, Color(Orange), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 10, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Evaluate Once(Global.fireSaleIndex))), 1, Visible To); // 15 elements
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 2 elements
                Create In-World Text(Local Player, Icon String(Question Mark), Add(Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array), Value In Array(Global.mysterylocations, Evaluate Once(Global.fireSaleIndex))), 2.25, Clip Against Surfaces, Visible To, Color(Orange), Default Visibility); // 16 elements
                Modify Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Append To Array, Last Text ID); // 2 elements
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Wait Until(Or(Compare(Global.fireSale, !=, True), Compare(Global.telespot, !=, Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 132))), 99999); // 16 elements
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 130)), 1); // 8 elements
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 130), Global.fireSaleIndex)); // 8 elements
        End; // 1 element
        For Global Variable(fireSaleIndex, 0, Count Of(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 131)), 1); // 8 elements
            Destroy In-World Text(Value In Array(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 131), Global.fireSaleIndex)); // 8 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 130, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 131, Empty Array); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(fireSaleBoxes, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

        Set Player Variable At Index(Event Player, _extendedPlayerCollection, 42, If-Then-Else(Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)), 1, -1)); // 10 elements

            Set Global Variable(Difficulty, Max(1, Min(5, Add(Global.Difficulty, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 42))))); // 15 elements
            Set Global Variable(difficultyStats, Array Slice(Value In Array(Global._extendedGlobalCollection, 30), Subtract(Multiply(Global.Difficulty, 6), 6), 6)); // 16 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, 1)); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(spawntype, Modulo(Add(Add(Global.spawntype, Value In Array((Event Player)._extendedPlayerCollection, 42)), 5), 5)); // 15 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, 2)); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(experiencesystem, Not(Global.experiencesystem)); // 3 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).menuPick, ==, 3)); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(settingsViewers, Null); // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Play Effect(Event Player, Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 40); // 3 elements
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
    }
}

    }
    actions {
        Wait Until(Compare(Server Load, <, 230), 3); // 6 elements
        If(Compare(Global.Bosssequencer, !=, 0)); // 6 elements
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} One champion at a time!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 7 elements
        Else If(Compare((Event Player).points, >=, Round To Integer(Subtract(Multiply(Multiply(15000, Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).creditcardlimit), To Nearest))); // 23 elements
            Modify Player Variable(Event Player, points, Subtract, Round To Integer(Multiply(Multiply(Multiply(15000, Raise To Power(0.5, (Event Player).coinUses)), Global.costmultiplier), (Event Player).cashback), To Nearest)); // 18 elements
            Set Global Variable(pricePerUse, 1); // 2 elements
            Call Subroutine(SpendRamattraCoins); // 1 element
            Chase Player Variable At Rate(Event Player, points2, Evaluate Once((Event Player).points), 15000, Destination and Rate); // 6 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(Team 1), Buff Impact Sound, Color(White), Event Player, 70); // 5 elements
            Set Global Variable(bossSpot, First Of(Sorted Array(Global.bossbuypositions, Distance Between(Event Player, Current Array Element)))); // 7 elements
            Set Global Variable(Bosssequencer, Add(Index Of Array Value(Global.bossbuypositions, Global.bossSpot), 1)); // 8 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Small Message(Event Player, Custom String("{0} You cannot afford to face my champion!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 7 elements
    }
}

        Set Global Variable At Index(_extendedGlobalCollection, 8, Null); // 2 elements
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(bossEffects, Empty Array); // 1 element

            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.bossIndex, Team 2), !=, Null)); // 8 elements
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.bossIndex); // 3 elements
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        Big Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("A champion approaches!")); // 7 elements
        Small Message(All Players(All Teams), Custom String("{0} You want a real fight? Face my champion!", Hero Icon String(Hero(Ramattra)))); // 9 elements
        Wait(2, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Skip(Value In Array(Array(5, 0, 2, 4), Add(Index Of Array Value(Array(1, 2, 3), Global.Bosssequencer), 1))); // 25 elements
        Call Subroutine(SpawnDevastator); // 1 element
        Skip(3); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(SpawnColossalCharger); // 1 element
        Skip(1); // 2 elements
        Call Subroutine(SpawnImperialWarmech); // 1 element
        //lbl_0:
        //lbl_1:
    }
}

        Ongoing - Each Player;

    actions {

            Start Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)); // 2 elements
        Else; // 1 element
            Stop Holding Button(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Wait(0.5, Abort When False); // 2 elements
        Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
    }
}

rule ("end boss fight") {
    event {
        Subroutine;
        EndBossFight;
    }
    actions {
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, 12, 1); // 4 elements
            If(Compare(Players In Slot(Global.bossIndex, Team 2), !=, Null)); // 8 elements
                Destroy Dummy Bot(Team 2, Global.bossIndex); // 3 elements
                Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Call Subroutine(LoadingPause); // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
        For Global Variable(bossIndex, 0, Count Of(Global.bossEffects), 1); // 5 elements
            Destroy Effect(Value In Array(Global.bossEffects, Global.bossIndex)); // 5 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(bossEffects, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(bossBots, Empty Array); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(bossBody, Null); // 1 element
        Set Global Variable(Bosssequencer, 0); // 2 elements
    }
}

//183 elements
rule ("289 Imperial warmech set attacks") {
    event {
        Ongoing - Each Player;
        Team 2;
        D.Va;
    }
    conditions {
        Global.Bosssequencer == 3; // 3 elements
        Event Player == Global.bossBody; // 2 elements
        Is Button Held(Event Player, Button(Primary Fire)) == True; // 4 elements
        Is Alive(Event Player) == True; // 2 elements
    }
    actions {
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, Major_Attacks, Random Integer(2, 6)); // 5 elements
        Wait(0.25, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        If(Compare((Event Player).Major_Attacks, ==, 2)); // 7 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Wrecking Ball Piledriver Impact Sound, Color(White), Eye Position(Event Player), 200); // 6 elements
            Play Effect(All Players(All Teams), Wrecking Ball Piledriver Impact Effect, Color(White), Position Of(Event Player), 1); // 6 elements
            Wait(0.75, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
        End; // 1 element
        Set Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackParts, Array(Array(Hero(Pharah), Vector(Empty Array, 5, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Zarya), Vector(Empty Array, 2, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Sojourn), Vector(Empty Array, 5, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Moira), Vector(Empty Array, 4, Empty Array)), Array(Hero(Junkrat), Vector(Empty Array, 4, -1)))); // 50 elements
        For Player Variable(Event Player, warmachAttackIndex, 0, Count Of((Event Player).warmachAttackParts), 1); // 6 elements
            If(Compare((Event Player).warmachAttackIndex, ==, Subtract((Event Player).Major_Attacks, 2))); // 11 elements
                Create Dummy Bot(First Of(Value In Array((Event Player).warmachAttackParts, (Event Player).warmachAttackIndex)), Team 2, -1, Event Player, Vector(0, 0, 0)); // 16 elements
                Modify Global Variable(bossBots, Append To Array, Last Created Entity); // 1 element
                Wait(0.016, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Set Status(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Null, Phased Out, 9999); // 4 elements
                Start Scaling Player(Last Of(Global.bossBots), 0.7, True); // 4 elements
                Attach Players(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Event Player, Multiply(Value In Array(Value In Array((Event Player).warmachAttackParts, (Event Player).warmachAttackIndex), 1), 0.7)); // 15 elements
                If(Compare((Event Player).Major_Attacks, ==, 3)); // 7 elements
                    Start Facing(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Add(Facing Direction Of(Event Player), Vector(Random Integer(-1, 1), Empty Array, Empty Array)), 1000, To Player, Direction and Turn Rate); // 14 elements
                Else; // 1 element
                    Start Facing(Last Of(Global.bossBots), Facing Direction Of(Event Player), 1000, To World, Direction and Turn Rate); // 5 elements
                End; // 1 element
                Wait(7, Ignore Condition); // 2 elements
                Loop If Condition Is True; // 1 element
            End; // 1 element
        End; // 1 element
    }
}

        Clear Status(Event Player, Burning); // 1 element
